4. Key Insights and Recommendations

4.1 Positive Aspects Customers Value

  • Customers consistently praise products for being skin, love, feel.
  • Common positive phrases like 'skin feel, sensitive skin, dry skin' indicate what customers appreciate.

4.2 Areas for Improvement

  • Negative reviews frequently mention skin, like, face.
  • The most common skincare problems reported are texture_issues, irritation, scent_issues.
  • Phrases like 'sensitive skin, feel like, break out' indicate customer pain points.

4.3 Product-Specific Recommendations

  • Trinity + Eye and Lip Enhancer Attachment Bundle: Address texture_issues, value issues based on customer feedback.
  • The Moisturizing Soft Cream Moisturizer: Address texture_issues, value issues based on customer feedback.
  • Rose Quartz Facial Roller: Address value, texture_issues issues based on customer feedback.
  • Algae + Moringa Universal Hydrating Face Oil: Address texture_issues, dryness issues based on customer feedback.
  • Mini Algae + Moringa Universal Face Oil: Address texture_issues, dryness issues based on customer feedback.
  • (Glow)Setting 100% Mineral Powder SPF 35: Address texture_issues, packaging issues based on customer feedback.
  • The Silk Serum Wrinkle-Smoothing Retinol Alternative: Address texture_issues, irritation issues based on customer feedback.
  • Absolue Soft Cream Revitalizing & Brightening Moisturizer: Address texture_issues, scent_issues issues based on customer feedback.
  • Lotus Balancing & Hydrating Natural Face Treatment Oil: Address texture_issues, breakouts issues based on customer feedback.
  • Turnaround Revitalizing Serum: Address texture_issues, breakouts issues based on customer feedback.
  • Rapid Radiance Set: Address texture_issues, scent_issues issues based on customer feedback.
  • Youth or Dare Multi-Acid & C-Serum: Address texture_issues, scent_issues issues based on customer feedback.
  • CLINICAL Discoloration Repair Serum: Address texture_issues, irritation issues based on customer feedback.
